<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

 A man travels $200 \ \text{miles}$ at an average speed of $60 \ \text{miles per hour}$.
 How long does the journey take in hours and minutes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex"> A man travels $200 \ \text{miles}$ at an average speed of $60 \ \text{miles per hour}$.
 How long does the journey take in hours and minutes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time taken}  &= \dfrac {200} {60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {20 \times 10} {20 \times 3} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {20} \times 10} {\cancel {20} \times 3} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {10} {3} \\\\
                    &= 3 \dfrac {1} {3} \ \text {hours} \\\\
                    &= 3 \ \text {hours} + 60 \times \dfrac {1} {3} \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                    &= 3 \ \text {hours} \ 20 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3 \ \text {hours} \ 20 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance_time
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two runners are having a race. Gary starts running from the start line at $10 \ \text{m/s}$. 
Two seconds later Andy starts running from the start line at $12 \ \text{m/s}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Two runners are having a race. Gary starts running from the start line at $10 \ \text{m/s}$. 
Two seconds later Andy starts running from the start line at $12 \ \text{m/s}$.
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How long after Gary starts running does Andy catch up with him?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How long after Gary starts running does Andy catch up with him?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the time taken by Gary to catch up (in seconds) $= t$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
12 \times (t - 2)  &=  10 \times t \\\\
12t - 24           &=  10t \\\\
12t - 10t          &=  24 \\\\
2t                 &=  24 \\\\
t                  &=  \dfrac{24}{2} \\\\
t                  &=  \dfrac{12 \times 2}{2} \\\\
t                  &=  \dfrac{12 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
t                  &=  12 \text{ seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

Time taken $= 12 \text{ seconds}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12 \text{ seconds}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A train leaves London and travels at a steady speed of $120 \ \text{mph}$ to Edinburgh. 
Before reaching Edinburgh, the train stops in Leeds after travelling for one and a half hours. 
The distance from London to Edinburgh is $420 \ \text{miles}$. Use this information to answer the following:

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A train leaves London and travels at a steady speed of $120 \ \text{mph}$ to Edinburgh. 
Before reaching Edinburgh, the train stops in Leeds after travelling for one and a half hours. 
The distance from London to Edinburgh is $420 \ \text{miles}$. Use this information to answer the following:
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How long does the train take to travel from London to Edinburgh?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How long does the train take to travel from London to Edinburgh?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}  &=  \dfrac{420 \text{ miles}}{120 \text{ mph}} \\\\
             &=  \dfrac{420}{120} \\\\
             &=  \dfrac{7 \times 6 \times 10}{2 \times 6 \times 10} \\\\
             &=  \dfrac{7 \times \cancel{6} \times \cancel{10}}{2 \times \cancel{6} \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
             &=  \dfrac{7}{2} \\\\
             &=  3.5 \text{ hours or } 3 \text{ hours and } 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3.5 \text{ hours or } 3 \text{ hours and } 30 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

tspeed
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Rio travels directly from $A$ to $C$ at a speed of $80 \ \text{km/hr}$.

Harry travels from $A$ to $B$ to $C$ at $100 \ \text{km/hr}$.

Fahad travels directly from $A$ to $C$ at $60 \ \text{km/hr}$.

![papers/bancrofts-school/2016/sample-paper-2/s01-q16-i00.png](/assets/papers/bancrofts-school/2016/sample-paper-2/s01-q16-i00.png "papers/bancrofts-school/2016/sample-paper-2/s01-q16-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Rio travels directly from $A$ to $C$ at a speed of $80 \ \text{km/hr}$.
<emptyline>
Harry travels from $A$ to $B$ to $C$ at $100 \ \text{km/hr}$.
<emptyline>
Fahad travels directly from $A$ to $C$ at $60 \ \text{km/hr}$.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How long (in hours and minutes) does each person take?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How long (in hours and minutes) does each person take?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Rio}   &=  \dfrac{200}{80} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{4 \times 5 \times 10}{4 \times 2 \times 10} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{\cancel{4} \times 5 \times \cancel{10}}{\cancel{4} \times 2 \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{5}{2} \text{ hours} \\\\
                           &=  2\dfrac{1}{2} \text{ hours} \\\\
                           &=  2 \text{ hours } 30 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Harry} &=  \dfrac{150 + 150}{100} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{300}{100} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{3 \times 100}{100} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{100}}{\cancel{100}} \\\\
                           &=  3 \text{ hours} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Fahad} &=  \dfrac{200}{60} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{2 \times 2 \times 5 \times 10}{3 \times 2 \times 10} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{2} \times 5 \times \cancel{10}}{3 \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{2 \times 5}{3} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{10}{3} \\\\
                           &=  3\dfrac{1}{3} \text{ hours} \\\\
                           &=  3 \text{ hours } 20 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Rio takes $2 \text{ hours } 30 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Harry takes $3 \text{ hours}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Fahad takes $3 \text{ hours } 20 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                           &=  \dfrac{4 \times 5 \times 10}{4 \times 2 \times 10} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{\cancel{4} \times 5 \times \cancel{10}}{\cancel{4} \times 2 \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{5}{2} \text{ hours} \\\\
                           &=  2\dfrac{1}{2} \text{ hours} \\\\
                           &=  2 \text{ hours } 30 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Harry} &=  \dfrac{150 + 150}{100} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{300}{100} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{3 \times 100}{100} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{100}}{\cancel{100}} \\\\
                           &=  3 \text{ hours} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Fahad} &=  \dfrac{200}{60} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{2 \times 2 \times 5 \times 10}{3 \times 2 \times 10} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{2} \times 5 \times \cancel{10}}{3 \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{2 \times 5}{3} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{10}{3} \\\\
                           &=  3\dfrac{1}{3} \text{ hours} \\\\
                           &=  3 \text{ hours } 20 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

**Question**

Guy is training for a long distance cycle race.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

He leaves home and cycles for $10 \ \text{km}$ at a speed of $30 \ \text{km per hour}$ . 

How many minutes does this take him?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">He leaves home and cycles for $10 \ \text{km}$ at a speed of $30 \ \text{km per hour}$ . 
<emptyline>
How many minutes does this take him?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}   &= \dfrac{10}{30} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{10}{10 \times 3} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{\cancel{10}}{\cancel{10} \times 3} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{1}{3} \text{ hours} \\\\
              &= 60 \times \dfrac{1}{3} \text{ minutes} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{60}{3} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{20 \times 3}{3} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{20 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
              &= 20 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

              &= 15 \text{ minutes}

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}             &= \dfrac{10 + 5}{30} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{15}{30} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{15}{15 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{\cancel{15}}{\cancel{15} \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{1}{2} \text{ hours} \\\\
                        &= 60 \times \dfrac{1}{2} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{60}{2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{30 \times 2}{2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{30 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                        &= 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$30 \text{ minutes}$

<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

tspeed

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A mouse is trying to climb out of a hole which is $220 \ \text{cm}$ deep.
Each hour, she manages to climb $40 \ \text{cm}$, but slips back $10 \ \text{cm}$ when she stops for a rest. 
How many hours will it take her to reach the top of the hole?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A mouse is trying to climb out of a hole which is $220 \ \text{cm}$ deep.
Each hour, she manages to climb $40 \ \text{cm}$, but slips back $10 \ \text{cm}$ when she stops for a rest. 
How many hours will it take her to reach the top of the hole?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}   &=  40 - 10 \\\\
               &=  30 \text{ cm per hour} \\\\
\text{Time}    &=  \dfrac{220}{30} \\\\
               &=  \dfrac{22\cancel{0}}{3\cancel{0}} \\\\
               &=  \dfrac{22}{3} \\\\
               &=  7\dfrac{1}{3} \text{ hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7\dfrac{1}{3} \text{ hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance_time
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}

               &=  30 \text{ cm per hour} \\\\
\text{Time}    &=  \dfrac{220}{30} \\\\
               &=  \dfrac{22\cancel{0}}{3\cancel{0}} \\\\
               &=  \dfrac{22}{3} \\\\
               &=  7\dfrac{1}{3} \text{ hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$7\dfrac{1}{3} \text{ hours}$

tspeed

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A train is $1 \ \text{km}$ long and travels through a tunnel which is $1.5 \ \text{km}$ long. 
If it travels at a constant speed of $60 \ \text{km/hr}$, how long will it take the whole train to pass through the tunnel?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A train is $1 \ \text{km}$ long and travels through a tunnel which is $1.5 \ \text{km}$ long. 
If it travels at a constant speed of $60 \ \text{km/hr}$, how long will it take the whole train to pass through the tunnel?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}     &= \dfrac{1 + 1.5}{60} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{2.5}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
                &= 60 \times \dfrac{2.5}{60} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{2.5 \times 60}{60} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{2.5 \times \cancel{60}}{\cancel{60}} \\\\
                &= 2.5 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2.5 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance_time
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

\text{Time}     &= \dfrac{1 + 1.5}{60} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{2.5}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
                &= 60 \times \dfrac{2.5}{60} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{2.5 \times 60}{60} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{2.5 \times \cancel{60}}{\cancel{60}} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="papername">
<p>city-of-london-school--11-plus--maths--2008--sample-paper-group-2:1:17</p>

<div class="topics">

<li>
tspeed
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I travel $1\ \text{mile}$ at $60\ \text{m.p.h.}$ and then $1\ \text{mile}$ at $30\ \text{m.p.h.}$ What is my average speed in miles per hour?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I travel $1\ \text{mile}$ at $60\ \text{m.p.h.}$ and then $1\ \text{mile}$ at $30\ \text{m.p.h.}$ What is my average speed in miles per hour?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Total distance}} {\text{Total time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total distance}  &= 1 + 1 \\\\
                        &= 2 \ \text{miles} \\\\
\text {Time 1}          &= \dfrac {1} {60} \ \text{hour} \\\\
\text {Time 2}          &= \dfrac {1} {30} \ \text{hour} \\\\
\text {Total time}      &= \dfrac {1} {60} + \dfrac {1} {30} \\\\
\text {Average speed}   &= \dfrac {2} {\dfrac {1} {60} + \dfrac {1} {30}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {\dfrac {1} {60} + \dfrac {2} {30 \times 2}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {\dfrac {1} {60} + \dfrac {2} {60}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {\dfrac {3} {60}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 60} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
                        &= 40 \ \text {m.p.h}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40 \ \text {m.p.h}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_average_speed
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total distance}  &= 1 + 1 \\\\
                        &= 2 \ \text{miles} \\\\
\text {Time 1}          &= \dfrac {1} {60} \ \text{hour} \\\\
\text {Time 2}          &= \dfrac {1} {30} \ \text{hour} \\\\
\text {Total time}      &= \dfrac {1} {60} + \dfrac {1} {30} \\\\
\text {Average speed}   &= \dfrac {2} {\dfrac {1} {60} + \dfrac {1} {30}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {\dfrac {1} {60} + \dfrac {2} {30 \times 2}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {\dfrac {1} {60} + \dfrac {2} {60}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {\dfrac {3} {60}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 60} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 20 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
                        &= 40 \ \text {m.p.h}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$40 \ \text {m.p.h}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>city-of-london-school--11-plus--maths--2020--sample-paper-1:1:10</p>

<div class="rag">

tspeed
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a sponsored walk: Adam took $4\ \text{hours}$, $39\ \text{minutes}$; 

Billy took $274\ \text{minutes}$ and Charlie took $4\dfrac{3}{5}\ \text{hours}$.

Who was the quickest and who was the slowest?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a sponsored walk: Adam took $4\ \text{hours}$, $39\ \text{minutes}$; 
<emptyline>
Billy took $274\ \text{minutes}$ and Charlie took $4\dfrac{3}{5}\ \text{hours}$.
<emptyline>
Who was the quickest and who was the slowest?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Adam}   &= 4 \text { hours } 39 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                            &= 4 \times 60 + 39 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                            &= 4 \times 60 + 39 \\\\
                            &= 240 + 39 \\\\
                            &= 279 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Billy}  &= 274 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Charlie}&= 4\dfrac{3}{5} \text { hours} \\\\
                            &= 4 \times 60 + \dfrac{3}{5} \times 60 \text { minutes} \\\\
                            &= 240 + \dfrac{3 \times 60}{5} \\\\
                            &= 240 + \dfrac{3 \times 12 \times 5}{5} \\\\
                            &= 240 + \dfrac{3 \times 12 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
                            &= 240 + 3 \times 12 \\\\
                            &= 240 + 36 \\\\
                            &= 276 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

Quickest = Billy

Slowest = Adam
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Billy and Adam

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Adam}   &= 4 \text { hours } 39 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                            &= 4 \times 60 + 39 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                            &= 4 \times 60 + 39 \\\\
                            &= 240 + 39 \\\\
                            &= 279 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Billy}  &= 274 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Charlie}&= 4\dfrac{3}{5} \text { hours} \\\\
                            &= 4 \times 60 + \dfrac{3}{5} \times 60 \text { minutes} \\\\
                            &= 240 + \dfrac{3 \times 60}{5} \\\\
                            &= 240 + \dfrac{3 \times 12 \times 5}{5} \\\\
                            &= 240 + \dfrac{3 \times 12 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
                            &= 240 + 3 \times 12 \\\\
                            &= 240 + 36 \\\\
                            &= 276 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Quickest = Billy
<emptyline>
Slowest = Adam
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Billy and Adam
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="papername">

<div class="rag">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram shows three different routes from Peter's factory (marked as $F$) to his warehouse (marked as $W$). 
Each section of the route is either motorway (labelled $M$), main road (labelled $A$) or minor road (labelled $B$).
The length of each section of the route is also stated.

![papers/csse/2016/sample-paper-3/s01-q10-i00.png](/assets/papers/csse/2016/sample-paper-3/s01-q10-i00.png "papers/csse/2016/sample-paper-3/s01-q10-i00.png")

Peter expects to travel at $90 \ \text{km/h}$ on the motorway, $60 \ \text{km/h}$ on the main road and $45 \ \text{km/h}$ 
on the minor road.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram shows three different routes from Peter's factory (marked as $F$) to his warehouse (marked as $W$). 
Each section of the route is either motorway (labelled $M$), main road (labelled $A$) or minor road (labelled $B$).
The length of each section of the route is also stated.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>

**Question**

How long, in minutes, will it take Peter to travel the first $15 \ \text{km}$ on the main road from the factory ($F$)?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How long, in minutes, will it take Peter to travel the first $15 \ \text{km}$ on the main road from the factory ($F$)?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}   &= \dfrac{15}{60} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{15}{15 \times 4} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{\cancel{15}}{\cancel{15} \times 4} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{1}{4} \text{ hours} \\\\
              &= 60 \times \dfrac{1}{4} \text{ minutes} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{60}{4} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{15 \times 4}{4} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{15 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
              &= 15 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$15 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How long, in minutes, will it take Peter to travel $45 \ \text{km}$ on the motorway (on route $I$)?
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}   &= \dfrac{45}{90} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{45}{45 \times 2} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{\cancel{45}}{\cancel{45} \times 2} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{1}{2} \text{ hours} \\\\
              &= 60 \times \dfrac{1}{2} \text{ minutes} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{60}{2} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{30 \times 2}{2} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{30 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
              &= 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$30 \text{ minutes}$

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How long, in hours and minutes, will it take Peter to complete the whole of route $II$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How long, in hours and minutes, will it take Peter to complete the whole of route $II$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken to cross main road}   &= \dfrac{15 + 60 + 30}{60} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{105}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
                                       &= 60 \times \dfrac{105}{60} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{60 \times 105}{60} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{\cancel{60} \times 105}{\cancel{60}} \\\\
                                       &= 105 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Time taken to cross minor road}  &= \dfrac{15}{45} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{15}{15 \times 3} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{\cancel{15}}{\cancel{15} \times 3} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{1}{3} \text{ hours} \\\\
                                       &= 60 \times \dfrac{1}{3} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{60}{3} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{20 \times 3}{3} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{20 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
                                       &= 20 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Total time taken}                &= 105 + 20 \\\\
                                       &= 125 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{125}{60} \text { hours} \\\\
                                       &= 2\dfrac{5}{60} \text { hours} \\\\
                                       &= 2 \text { hours } 5 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \text { hours } 5 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance_time
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

                                       &= \dfrac{105}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
                                       &= 60 \times \dfrac{105}{60} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{60 \times 105}{60} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{\cancel{60} \times 105}{\cancel{60}} \\\\
                                       &= 105 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Time taken to cross minor road}  &= \dfrac{15}{45} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{15}{15 \times 3} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{\cancel{15}}{\cancel{15} \times 3} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{1}{3} \text{ hours} \\\\
                                       &= 60 \times \dfrac{1}{3} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{60}{3} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{20 \times 3}{3} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{20 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
                                       &= 20 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Total time taken}                &= 105 + 20 \\\\
                                       &= 125 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                       &= \dfrac{125}{60} \text { hours} \\\\
                                       &= 2\dfrac{5}{60} \text { hours} \\\\
                                       &= 2 \text { hours } 5 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

A Formula $1$ race is $96$ laps of the track. A driver averages $1 \ \text{minute} \ 20 \ \text{seconds}$ per lap. How long is his race in hours and minutes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A Formula $1$ race is $96$ laps of the track. A driver averages $1 \ \text{minute} \ 20 \ \text{seconds}$ per lap. How long is his race in hours and minutes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Race duration}   &= 96 \times (1 \text{ minute } 20 \text{ seconds} ) \\\\
                        &= 96 \times (60 \text{ seconds} + 20 \text{ seconds} ) \\\\
                        &= 96 \times 80 \text{ seconds} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {96 \times 80} {60} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {12 \times 8 \times 16 \times 5} {12 \times 5}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel{12} \times 8 \times 16 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{12} \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
                        &= 128 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                        &= 2 \ \text {hours} \ 8 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \ \text {hour} \ 8 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

\begin{aligned}
\text {Race duration}   &= 96 \times (1 \text{ minute } 20 \text{ seconds} ) \\\\
                        &= 96 \times (60 \text{ seconds} + 20 \text{ seconds} ) \\\\
                        &= 96 \times 80 \text{ seconds} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {96 \times 80} {60} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {12 \times 8 \times 16 \times 5} {12 \times 5}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel{12} \times 8 \times 16 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{12} \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
                        &= 128 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                        &= 2 \ \text {hours} \ 8 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The same coach takes $3 \ \text{h}$ to get to Bristol, a journey of $120 \ \text{miles}$. What is its average speed in $\text{miles per hour}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The same coach takes $3 \ \text{h}$ to get to Bristol, a journey of $120 \ \text{miles}$. What is its average speed in $\text{miles per hour}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Speed}    &= \dfrac {120 \ \text {miles}} {3 \ \text {hour}} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {3 \times 40 } {3} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac { \cancel 3 \times 40} {\cancel 3} \\\\
                 &= 40 \ \text {miles/hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40 \ \text {miles/hour}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Speed}    &= \dfrac {120 \ \text {miles}} {3 \ \text {hour}} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {3 \times 40 } {3} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac { \cancel 3 \times 40} {\cancel 3} \\\\
                 &= 40 \ \text {miles/hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Rhys and Sarah are on their bicycles and start $90 \ \text{miles}$ apart on a road, riding towards each other. 
Rhys is travelling at $10 \ \text{mph}$ and Sarah is travelling at $20 \ \text{mph}$. They set off at the same time and both keep moving at constant speeds until they meet.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Rhys and Sarah are on their bicycles and start $90 \ \text{miles}$ apart on a road, riding towards each other. 
Rhys is travelling at $10 \ \text{mph}$ and Sarah is travelling at $20 \ \text{mph}$. They set off at the same time and both keep moving at constant speeds until they meet.
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out at what distance each rider has cycled by the time they meet.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out at what distance each rider has cycled by the time they meet.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Rhys and Sarah meet at time (in hours) $= t$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
10 \times t + 20 \times t &= 90 \\\\
10t + 20t                 &= 90 \\\\
30t                       &= 90 \\\\
t                         &= \dfrac{90}{30} \\\\
t                         &= \dfrac{3 \times 30}{30} \\\\
t                         &= \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{30}}{\cancel{30}} \\\\
t                         &= 3 \text{ hours}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance travelled by Rhys}  &= 10 \times t \\\\
                                   &= 10 \times 3 \\\\
                                   &= 30 \text { miles} \\\\
\text{Distance travelled by Sarah} &= 20 \times t \\\\
                                   &= 20 \times 3 \\\\
                                   &= 60 \text { miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$30 \text { miles}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60 \text { miles}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let Rhys and Sarah meet at time (in hours) $= t$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
10 \times t + 20 \times t &= 90 \\\\
10t + 20t                 &= 90 \\\\
30t                       &= 90 \\\\
t                         &= \dfrac{90}{30} \\\\
t                         &= \dfrac{3 \times 30}{30} \\\\
t                         &= \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{30}}{\cancel{30}} \\\\
t                         &= 3 \text{ hours}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance travelled by Rhys}  &= 10 \times t \\\\
                                   &= 10 \times 3 \\\\
                                   &= 30 \text { miles} \\\\
\text{Distance travelled by Sarah} &= 20 \times t \\\\
                                   &= 20 \times 3 \\\\
                                   &= 60 \text { miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If a tourist cycles at $14 \ \text{km per hour}$, work out how long her journey would take from Josham to Moreton in hours and minutes.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If a tourist cycles at $14 \ \text{km per hour}$, work out how long her journey would take from Josham to Moreton in hours and minutes.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Distance}    &= \text {Josham to Langham} + \text {Langham to Moreton} \\\\
                    &= 26 + 9 \\\\
                    &= 35 \ \text {km} \\\\
\text {Time}        &= \dfrac {35} {14} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {7 \times 5} {7 \times 2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{7} \times 5} {\cancel{7} \times 2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {5} {2} \ \text {hours} \\\\
                    &= 2 \ \text {hours} \ 30 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \ \text {hours} \ 30 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

An aeroplane takes $7 \ \text{hours}$ to travel from London to New York at a
constant speed. The distance from London to New York is approximately $5600 \ \text{kilometres}$. 
Calculate the approximate speed of the aeroplane in kilometres per hour.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

constant speed. The distance from London to New York is approximately $5600 \ \text{kilometres}$. 
Calculate the approximate speed of the aeroplane in kilometres per hour.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Speed}   &= \dfrac {5600} {7} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {800 \times 7} {7} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {800 \times \cancel {7} } {\cancel {7} } \\\\
                &= 800 \ \text {kilometres per hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

tspeed

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

James cycles $4000 \ \text{m}$ at $10 \ \text{metres per second}$. Tom takes $100 \ \text{seconds}$ more to cycle the same distance. 
At what speed does Tom cycle? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">James cycles $4000 \ \text{m}$ at $10 \ \text{metres per second}$. Tom takes $100 \ \text{seconds}$ more to cycle the same distance. 
At what speed does Tom cycle? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by James}      &= \dfrac{4000}{10} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{400 \cancel{0}}{1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                                &= 400 \text{ seconds} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Tom}        &= 100 + 400 \\\\
                                &= 500 \text{ seconds} \\\\
\text{Speed for Tom}            &= \dfrac{4000}{500} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{5 \times 8 \times 100}{5 \times 100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 8 \times \cancel{100}}{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{100}} \\\\
                                &= 8 \text{ metres per second}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8 \text{ metres per second}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_speed
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

                                &= \dfrac{400 \cancel{0}}{1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                                &= 400 \text{ seconds} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Tom}        &= 100 + 400 \\\\
                                &= 500 \text{ seconds} \\\\
\text{Speed for Tom}            &= \dfrac{4000}{500} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{5 \times 8 \times 100}{5 \times 100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 8 \times \cancel{100}}{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{100}} \\\\
                                &= 8 \text{ metres per second}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-g/s01-q26-i00.png](/assets/papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-g/s01-q26-i00.png "papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-g/s01-q26-i00.png")

Calum and his friend cycle from $A$ to $C$, passing through $B$.

During the trip he asks his friend how far they have cycled.

His friend replies "one third as far as it is from here to $B$".

Ten miles later Calum asks him how far they still have to cycle to reach $C$.

His friend replies again "one third as far as it is from here to $B$".

How far from $A$ will Calum have cycled when he reaches $C?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<emptyline>

<emptyline>

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 6 \ \text{metres per second} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \ \text{metres}} {1 \ \text{second}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 } {1000} \ \text{kilometres} \times \dfrac {60 \times 60} {1 \ \text{hour}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times 60 \times 60} {1000} \ \text{kilometres per hour} \\\\
&= \dfrac {21600} {1000} \ \text{kilometres per hour} \\\\
&= 21.6 \ \text{kilometres per hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Kasey takes $24 \ \text{minutes}$ to cycle from $A$ to $B$.

Shayban cycles at three quarters of Kasey's speed.

How long does it take Shayban to cycle from $A$ to $B?$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Kasey takes $24 \ \text{minutes}$ to cycle from $A$ to $B$.
<emptyline>
Shayban cycles at three quarters of Kasey's speed.
<emptyline>
How long does it take Shayban to cycle from $A$ to $B?$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the distance $= D$

Speed $= \dfrac{D}{24}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed of Shyaban}      &= \dfrac{1}{3} \text{ of } \dfrac{D}{24} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac{1}{3} \times \dfrac{D}{24} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac{D}{24 \times 3} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac{D}{72} \\\\
\text{Time}                  &= \dfrac{D}{\dfrac{D}{72}} \\\\
                             &= 72 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$72 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                             &= \dfrac{D}{72} \\\\

tspeed
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

James is driving  on the M25. He is annoyed because a $35  \ \text{mile}$ stretch of road works has a speed restriction of $50 \ \text{mph}$ rather being able to travel at $70 \ \text{mph}$.

How much longer is his journey as a result of the roadworks?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">James is driving  on the M25. He is annoyed because a $35  \ \text{mile}$ stretch of road works has a speed restriction of $50 \ \text{mph}$ rather being able to travel at $70 \ \text{mph}$.
<emptyline>
How much longer is his journey as a result of the roadworks?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the time taken (without restriction) $= t$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
t                   &= \dfrac{35}{70} \\\\
t                   &= \dfrac{35}{35 \times 2} \\\\
t                   &= \dfrac{\cancel{35}}{\cancel{35} \times 2} \\\\
t                   &= \dfrac{1}{2} \\\\
t                   &= 0.5 \text{ hour} \\\\
\text{Time}         &= \dfrac{35}{50} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 7}{\cancel{5} \times 10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{7}{10} \\\\
                    &= 0.7 \text{ hour} \\\\
\text{Extra time}   &= 0.7 - t \\\\
                    &= 0.7 - 0.5 \\\\
                    &= 0.2 \text{ hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$0.2 \text{ hour}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance_time
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the time taken (without restriction) $= t$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
t                   &= \dfrac{35}{70} \\\\
t                   &= \dfrac{35}{35 \times 2} \\\\
t                   &= \dfrac{\cancel{35}}{\cancel{35} \times 2} \\\\
t                   &= \dfrac{1}{2} \\\\
t                   &= 0.5 \text{ hour} \\\\
\text{Time}         &= \dfrac{35}{50} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 7}{\cancel{5} \times 10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{7}{10} \\\\
                    &= 0.7 \text{ hour} \\\\
\text{Extra time}   &= 0.7 - t \\\\
                    &= 0.7 - 0.5 \\\\
                    &= 0.2 \text{ hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>

</div>

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$0.2 \text{ hour}$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Issac cycles for $20 \ \text{minutes}$ at $18  \ \text{km/hr}$ and for $10 \ \text{minutes}$ at $6 \ \text{km/hr}$.
How far has he cycled and what is his average speed? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Issac cycles for $20 \ \text{minutes}$ at $18  \ \text{km/hr}$ and for $10 \ \text{minutes}$ at $6 \ \text{km/hr}$.
How far has he cycled and what is his average speed? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance covered}  &= 20 \text{ minute} \times 18 \text{ km/hr} + 10 \text { minute} \times 6 \text{ km/hr} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{20}{60} \text{ hour} \times 18 \text{ km/hr} + \dfrac{10}{60} \text { hour} \times 6 \text{ km/hr} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{20}{60} \times 18 + \dfrac{10}{60} \times 6 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{20 \times 18}{60} + \dfrac{10 \times 6}{60} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{20 \times 6 \times 3}{20 \times 3} + \dfrac{10 \times 6}{10 \times 6} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{\cancel{20} \times 6 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{20} \times \cancel{3}} + \dfrac{\cancel{10} \times \cancel{6}}{\cancel{10} \times \cancel{6}} \\\\
                         &= 6 + 1 \\\\
                         &= 7 \text{ km} \\\\
\text{Average speed}     &= \dfrac{7 \text{ km}}{20 + 10 \text{ minutes}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{7 \text{ km}}{30 \text{ minutes}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{7 \times 60 \text{ km}}{30 \text{ hours}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{7 \times 60}{30} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{7 \times 30 \times 2}{30} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{7 \times \cancel{30} \times 2}{\cancel{30}} \\\\
                         &= 7 \times 2 \\\\
                         &= 14 \text{ km/hr}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7 \text{ km}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$14 \text{ km/hr}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                         &= \dfrac{20}{60} \times 18 + \dfrac{10}{60} \times 6 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{20 \times 18}{60} + \dfrac{10 \times 6}{60} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{20 \times 6 \times 3}{20 \times 3} + \dfrac{10 \times 6}{10 \times 6} \\\\

                         &= 7 \text{ km} \\\\
\text{Average speed}     &= \dfrac{7 \text{ km}}{20 + 10 \text{ minutes}} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Chrystine's candle is $42 \ \text{cm}$ tall and burns at a rate of $3 \ \text{cm}$ every $30 \ \text{minutes}$.
Kieran's candle is $30 \ \text{cm}$ tall and burns at a rate of $2 \ \text{cm}$ every $40 \ \text{minutes}$.

They both light their candles at the same time.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

Kieran's candle is $30 \ \text{cm}$ tall and burns at a rate of $2 \ \text{cm}$ every $40 \ \text{minutes}$.
<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
