<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much longer does Kieran's candle take to burn completely down than Chrystine's candle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much longer does Kieran's candle take to burn completely down than Chrystine's candle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Burn rate for Chrystine}   &= \dfrac{3}{30} \text { cm per minute} \\\\
\text{Time taken }               &= \dfrac {42} { \dfrac{3}{30} } \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{42 \times 30}{3} \\\\ 
                                 &= \dfrac{42 \times 3 \times 10}{3} \\\\ 
                                 &= \dfrac{42 \times \cancel{3} \times 10}{\cancel{3}} \\\\ 
                                 &= 42 \times 10 \\\\ 
                                 &= 420 \text{ minutes} \\\\ 
\text{Burn rate for Kieran}      &= \dfrac{2}{40} \text { cm per minute} \\\\
\text{Time taken }               &= \dfrac {30} { \dfrac{2}{40} } \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{30 \times 40}{2} \\\\ 
                                 &= \dfrac{30 \times 20 \times 2}{2} \\\\ 
                                 &= \dfrac{30 \times 20 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\ 
                                 &= 30 \times 20 \\\\ 
                                 &= 600 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Extra time}                &= 600 - 420 \\\\
                                 &= 180 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{180}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{3 \times 60}{60} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{60}}{\cancel{60}} \\\\
                                 &= 3 \text{ hours}
\end{aligned}
$

Kieran's candle takes $3$ hours longer to burn completely down than Chrystine's candle.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3 \text{ hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance_time
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Burn rate for Chrystine}   &= \dfrac{3}{30} \text { cm per minute} \\\\
\text{Time taken }               &= \dfrac {42} { \dfrac{3}{30} } \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{42 \times 30}{3} \\\\ 
                                 &= \dfrac{42 \times 3 \times 10}{3} \\\\ 
                                 &= \dfrac{42 \times \cancel{3} \times 10}{\cancel{3}} \\\\ 
                                 &= 42 \times 10 \\\\ 
                                 &= 420 \text{ minutes} \\\\ 
\text{Burn rate for Kieran}      &= \dfrac{2}{40} \text { cm per minute} \\\\
\text{Time taken }               &= \dfrac {30} { \dfrac{2}{40} } \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{30 \times 40}{2} \\\\ 
                                 &= \dfrac{30 \times 20 \times 2}{2} \\\\ 
                                 &= \dfrac{30 \times 20 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\ 
                                 &= 30 \times 20 \\\\ 
                                 &= 600 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Extra time}                &= 600 - 420 \\\\
                                 &= 180 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{180}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{3 \times 60}{60} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{60}}{\cancel{60}} \\\\
                                 &= 3 \text{ hours}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Kieran's candle takes $3$ hours longer to burn completely down than Chrystine's candle.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$3 \text{ hours}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

After how many minutes were the candles the same height?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">After how many minutes were the candles the same height?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let both candles are the same height at time (in minutes) $= t$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
42 - \dfrac{3}{30} \times t                                          &= 30 - \dfrac{2}{40} \times t \\\\
42 - \dfrac{3t}{30}                                                  &= 30 - \dfrac{2t}{40} \\\\
42 - \dfrac{t}{10}                                                   &= 30 - \dfrac{t}{20} \\\\
30 - \dfrac{t}{20}                                                   &= 42 - \dfrac{t}{10} \\\\
\dfrac{t}{10} - \dfrac{t}{20}                                        &= 42 - 30 \\\\
\dfrac{2 \times t}{2 \times 10} - \dfrac{t}{20}                      &= 12 \\\\
\dfrac{2t}{20} - \dfrac{t}{20}                                       &= 12 \\\\
\dfrac{2t - t}{20}                                                   &= 12 \\\\
\dfrac{t}{20}                                                        &= 12 \\\\
t                                                                    &= 12 \times 20 \\\\
t                                                                    &= 240 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

Time $= 240 \text{ minutes}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$240 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let both candles are the same height at time (in minutes) $= t$
<emptyline>

$
\begin{aligned}
42 - \dfrac{3}{30} \times t                                          &= 30 - \dfrac{2}{40} \times t \\\\
42 - \dfrac{3t}{30}                                                  &= 30 - \dfrac{2t}{40} \\\\
42 - \dfrac{t}{10}                                                   &= 30 - \dfrac{t}{20} \\\\
30 - \dfrac{t}{20}                                                   &= 42 - \dfrac{t}{10} \\\\
\dfrac{t}{10} - \dfrac{t}{20}                                        &= 42 - 30 \\\\
\dfrac{2 \times t}{2 \times 10} - \dfrac{t}{20}                      &= 12 \\\\
\dfrac{2t}{20} - \dfrac{t}{20}                                       &= 12 \\\\
\dfrac{2t - t}{20}                                                   &= 12 \\\\
\dfrac{t}{20}                                                        &= 12 \\\\
t                                                                    &= 12 \times 20 \\\\
t                                                                    &= 240 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Time $= 240 \text{ minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>

tspeed
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Daisy the cow takes $1\ \text{hour}$ to walk $4\ \text{km}$.
Silver Blaze the horse walks $3$ times as fast as Daisy. 
At this speed how long would it take Silver Blaze to walk $4\ \text{km}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Daisy the cow takes $1\ \text{hour}$ to walk $4\ \text{km}$.
Silver Blaze the horse walks $3$ times as fast as Daisy. 
At this speed how long would it take Silver Blaze to walk $4\ \text{km}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed of Daisy} &= \dfrac{4} {1} \ \text{km per hour} \\
                      &= 4 \ \text{km per hour} \\
\text{Speed of Silver Blaze} &= 3 \times 4 \ \text{km per hour} \\
                             &= 12 \ \text{km per hour} \\
\text{Time taken by Silver Blaze} &= \dfrac{\text{distance}}{\text{speed}} \\
                                  &= \dfrac{4} {12} \ \text{hours} \\
                                  &= \dfrac{1} {3} \ \text{hour} \\
                                  &= \dfrac{1} {3} \times 60 \ \text{minutes} \\
                                  &= 20 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \ \text{minutes}$


</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}

                      &= 4 \ \text{km per hour} \\
\text{Speed of Silver Blaze} &= 3 \times 4 \ \text{km per hour} \\
                             &= 12 \ \text{km per hour} \\
\text{Time taken by Silver Blaze} &= \dfrac{\text{distance}}{\text{speed}} \\
                                  &= \dfrac{4} {12} \ \text{hours} \\
                                  &= \dfrac{1} {3} \ \text{hour} \\
                                  &= \dfrac{1} {3} \times 60 \ \text{minutes} \\
                                  &= 20 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A cyclist cycles $45 \ \text{kilometres}$ in $3 \ \text{hours}$. 
How many minutes does it take him to cycle $1500 \ \text{metres}$ at the same rate? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A cyclist cycles $45 \ \text{kilometres}$ in $3 \ \text{hours}$. 
How many minutes does it take him to cycle $1500 \ \text{metres}$ at the same rate? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}  &= \dfrac{45}{3} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{15 \times 3}{3} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{15 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
              &= 15 \text{ kilometres/hour} \\\\
              &= 15 \times \dfrac{1000}{60} \text{ metres/minute} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{15 \times 1000}{60} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{15 \times 250 \times 4}{15 \times 4} \\\\
              &= \dfrac{\cancel{15} \times 250 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{15} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
              &= 250 \text{ metres/minute}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}  &=  \dfrac{1500}{250} \\\\
                   &=  \dfrac{150\cancel{0}}{25\cancel{0}} \\\\
                   &=  \dfrac{150}{25} \\\\
                   &=  \dfrac{5 \times 5 \times 6}{5 \times 5} \\\\
                   &=  \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{5} \times 6}{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
                   &=  6 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Alternative solution

Let the time taken in minutes $= t$

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{1500}{t}  &= \dfrac{45 \text{ kilometres}}{3 \text{ hours}} \\\\
\dfrac{1500}{t}  &= \dfrac{45 \times 1000 \text{ metres}}{3 \times 60 \text{ minutes}} \\\\
\dfrac{1500}{t}  &= \dfrac{45 \times 1000}{3 \times 60}\\\\
\dfrac{1500}{t}  &= \dfrac{45000}{180}  \\\\
t                &= \dfrac{180 \times 1500}{45000}  \\\\
t                &= \dfrac{3 \times 6 \times 10 \times 15 \times 100}{3 \times 15 \times 10 \times 100}  \\\\
t                &= \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 6 \times \cancel{10} \times \cancel{15} \times \cancel{100}}{\cancel{3} \times \cancel{15} \times \cancel{10} \times \cancel{100}} \\\\
t                &= 6 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\end{aligned}
$

$

\dfrac{1500}{t}  &= \dfrac{45 \text{ kilometres}}{3 \text{ hours}} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A train travels $80\ \text{km}$ in $24 \ \text{minutes}$. How long will it take to travel $150\ \text{km}?$ 

- $48\ \text{minutes}$

- $\dfrac{1}{2}\ \text{hour}$

- $42\ \text{minutes}$

- $1\ \text{hour}$

- $45\ \text{minutes}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A train travels $80\ \text{km}$ in $24 \ \text{minutes}$. How long will it take to travel $150\ \text{km}?$ 
<emptyline>
- $48\ \text{minutes}$
<emptyline>
- $\dfrac{1}{2}\ \text{hour}$
<emptyline>
- $42\ \text{minutes}$
<emptyline>
- $1\ \text{hour}$
<emptyline>
- $45\ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}       &= \dfrac{80}{24} \text{ km/minute} \\\\
\text{Time taken}  &= \dfrac{150}{\dfrac{80}{24}} \\\\
                   &= \dfrac{150 \times 24}{80} \\\\
                   &= \dfrac{15 \times 10 \times 3 \times 8}{8 \times 10} \\\\
                   &= \dfrac{15 \times \cancel{10} \times 3 \times \cancel{8}}{\cancel{8} \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                   &=  15 \times 3 \\\\
                   &=  45 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$45 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

$

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mr Sahota is cycling around a track at a constant speed. He completes $5$ laps every $4\ \text{minutes}$. How long will it take him to complete $8$ laps? Give your answer in minutes and seconds.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Mr Sahota is cycling around a track at a constant speed. He completes $5$ laps every $4\ \text{minutes}$. How long will it take him to complete $8$ laps? Give your answer in minutes and seconds.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}                            &=  \dfrac{5 \text{ laps}}{4 \text{ minutes}}  \\\\
                                        &=  \dfrac{5}{4} \text{ laps/minute}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken to complete 8 laps}    &=  \dfrac{8}{\dfrac{5}{4}} \\\\
                                        &=  8 \times \dfrac{4}{5} \\\\
                                        &=  \dfrac{8 \times 4}{5} \\\\
                                        &=  \dfrac{32}{5} \\\\
                                        &=  6\dfrac{2}{5} \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                        &=  6 \text{ minutes } 24 \text { seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \text{ minutes } 24 \text { seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

It takes Margaret $45\ \text{minutes}$ to cycle $9\ \text{km}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">It takes Margaret $45\ \text{minutes}$ to cycle $9\ \text{km}$.
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How long would it take her to cycle $1\ \text{km}$ at that speed?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How long would it take her to cycle $1\ \text{km}$ at that speed?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}      &=  \dfrac{9}{45} \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{9}{9 \times 5} \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{\cancel{9}}{\cancel{9} \times 5} \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{1}{5} \text{ km/minute} \\\\
\text{Time taken} &=  \dfrac{1}{\dfrac{1}{5}} \\\\
                  &=  5 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

                  &=  \dfrac{9}{9 \times 5} \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{\cancel{9}}{\cancel{9} \times 5} \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{1}{5} \text{ km/minute} \\\\

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How far will she have travelled in one hour at that speed?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance travelled}  &= \dfrac{1}{5} \times 1 \text{ hour} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac{1}{5} \times (1 \times 60) \text{ minutes} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac{1}{5} \times 60 \\\\
                           &= \dfrac{60}{5} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac{12 \times 5}{5} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac{12 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
                           &= 12 \text{ km}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12 \text{ km}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Josh can walk at $6 \ \text{km}$ per hour. How long will he take to finish the walk?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Josh can walk at $6 \ \text{km}$ per hour. How long will he take to finish the walk?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Josh}                           &= \dfrac {36} {6} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {6 \times 6} {6} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{6} \times 6} {\cancel{6}} \\\\
                                                    &= 6 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \ \text{hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>

                                                    &= 6 \ \text{hours}

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Gary can walk at $5 \ \text{km}$ per hour. How far will he still have left to walk when Josh finishes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Gary can walk at $5 \ \text{km}$ per hour. How far will he still have left to walk when Josh finishes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Remaining distance}                           &= \text{Total distance} - \text{Distance covered} \\\\
                                                    &= 36  - 5  \times 6 \\\\
                                                    &= 36 - 30 \\\\
                                                    &= 6 \ \text{km}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \ \text{km}$

</div>

                                                    &= 36 - 30 \\\\

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Julie can walk at $6 \ \text{km}$ per hour. How long will she take to finish the walk? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Julie can walk at $6 \ \text{km}$ per hour. How long will she take to finish the walk? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Julie}                          &= \dfrac {24} {6} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {6 \times 4} {6} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{6} \times 4} {\cancel{6}} \\\\
                                                    &= 4 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \ \text{hours}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Sarika can walk at $4 \ \text{km}$ per hour. How far will she still have left to walk when Julie finishes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Sarika can walk at $4 \ \text{km}$ per hour. How far will she still have left to walk when Julie finishes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Remaining distance}                           &= \text{Total distance} - \text{Distance covered} \\\\
                                                    &= 24  - 4  \times 4 \\\\
                                                    &= 24 - 16 \\\\
                                                    &= 8 \ \text{km}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8 \ \text{km}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What was his average speed home again if it takes him $2\dfrac{1}{2} \ \text{hours}$ to get back?                                            

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What was his average speed home again if it takes him $2\dfrac{1}{2} \ \text{hours}$ to get back?                                            
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Total distance}} {\text{Total time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Average speed}   &=  \dfrac{100}{2\dfrac{1}{2}} \\\\
                       &=  \dfrac{100}{\dfrac{5}{2}} \\\\
                       &=  \dfrac{100 \times 2}{5} \\\\
                       &=  \dfrac{20 \times 5 \times 2}{5} \\\\
                       &=  \dfrac{20 \times \cancel{5} \times 2}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
                       &=  20 \times 2 \\\\
                       &=  40 \text{ km/h} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40 \text{ km/h}$

</div>
</div>

tspeed
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Matthew reads at an average rate of $30 \ \text{pages per hour}$, while Alex reads at an average rate of $40 \ \text{pages per hour}$. If Matthew starts reading a novel at $4{:}30 \ \text{PM}$, and Alex begins reading the same novel at $5{:}20 \ \text{PM}$, at what time will they be reading the same page?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Matthew reads at an average rate of $30 \ \text{pages per hour}$, while Alex reads at an average rate of $40 \ \text{pages per hour}$. If Matthew starts reading a novel at $4{:}30 \ \text{PM}$, and Alex begins reading the same novel at $5{:}20 \ \text{PM}$, at what time will they be reading the same page?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the time taken (in minutes) by Matthew $= t$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
40 \times (t - 50) \text{ minutes}                &=  30 \times t \text{ minutes} \\\\
40 \times \dfrac{(t - 50)}{60} \text{ hours}      &=  30 \times \dfrac{t}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
\dfrac{40 \times (t - 50)}{60}                    &= \dfrac{30 \times t}{60} \\\\
\dfrac{40t - 2000}{60}                            &= \dfrac{30t}{60} \\\\
\dfrac{40t - 2000}{60} - \dfrac{30t}{60}          &= 0 \\\\
\dfrac{40t - 2000 - 30t}{60}                      &= 0 \\\\
\dfrac{10t - 2000}{60}                            &= 0 \\\\
10t - 2000                                        &= 60 \times 0 \\\\
10t - 2000                                        &= 0 \\\\
10t                                               &= 2000 \\\\
t                                                 &= \dfrac{2000}{10} \\\\
t                                                 &= \dfrac{200 \times 10}{10} \\\\
t                                                 &= \dfrac{200 \times \cancel{10}}{\cancel{10}} \\\\
t                                                 &= 200 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}   &=   4{:}30 + 200 \text{ minutes} \\\\
              &=   7{:}50
\end{aligned}
$

At $7{:}50$, they will be reading the same page.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7{:}50$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the time taken (in minutes) by Matthew $= t$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                  &=  90 \text{ m}

**Question**

$11 \text{ hours } 15  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$11 \text{ hours } 15  \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance}   &=  12 \times (11 \text{ hours } 15  \ \text{minutes}) \\\\
                  &=  12 \times 11\dfrac{15}{60} \\\\
                  &=  12 \times 11\dfrac{15}{60} \\\\
                  &=  12 \times \dfrac{11 \times 60 + 15}{60}  \\\\
                  &=  12 \times \dfrac{660 + 15}{60}  \\\\
                  &=  12 \times \dfrac{675}{60}  \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{12 \times 675}{60}  \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{12 \times 135 \times 5}{12 \times 5}  \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{\cancel{12} \times 135 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{12} \times \cancel{5}}  \\\\
                  &=  135 \text{ m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$135 \text{ m}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

James starts a journey at $10.00 \ \text{am}$ and drives at $60 \ \text{mph}$ until $1.00 \ \text{pm}$. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>

\end{aligned}
$

- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>

                    &= 60 \text{ m}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Total distance}} {\text{Total time}}$
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                       &= \dfrac{48 \times 5}{5} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{48 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
                       &= 48 \text{ mph}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A man cycles from Birmingham to Leicester, which is $44 \ \text{miles}$ away. He starts at $9.00 \ \text{am}$ and cycles at $9  \ \text{miles per hour}$ until $1.00  \ \text{pm}$ when he has a puncture. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A man cycles from Birmingham to Leicester, which is $44 \ \text{miles}$ away. He starts at $9.00 \ \text{am}$ and cycles at $9  \ \text{miles per hour}$ until $1.00  \ \text{pm}$ when he has a puncture. 
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How far has he gone?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How far has he gone?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}  &= 1{:}00 - 9{:}00 \\\\
                   &= 4 \text{ hours} \\\\
\text{Distance}    &= 9 \times 4 \\\\
                   &= 36 \text{ miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$36 \text{ miles}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

He walks the rest of the way to Leicester and arrives at $5.00  \ \text{pm}$. What is his average speed for this section of the journey 
if he started walking at $1.00  \ \text{pm?}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">He walks the rest of the way to Leicester and arrives at $5.00  \ \text{pm}$. What is his average speed for this section of the journey 
if he started walking at $1.00  \ \text{pm?}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Total distance}} {\text{Total time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}       &= 5{:}00 - 1{:}00 \\\\
                        &= 4 \text{ hours} \\\\
\text{Average speed}    &= \dfrac{8}{4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{2 \times 4}{4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
                        &= 2 \text{ miles per hour} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \text{ miles per hour}$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">He catches a train back from Leicester to Birmingham, and travels at $66 \ \text{miles per hour}$. How long does the return journey take?
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}  &=  \dfrac{44}{66} \\\\

                   &=  \dfrac{2}{3} \text{ hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$\dfrac{2}{3} \text{ hours}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

It takes Katie $3 \ \text{hours}$ to jog $18  \ \text{miles}$ at a steady pace. If Katie decides to run for only two hours, but at the same speed how far would she run? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">It takes Katie $3 \ \text{hours}$ to jog $18  \ \text{miles}$ at a steady pace. If Katie decides to run for only two hours, but at the same speed how far would she run? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}   &=  \dfrac{18}{3} \\\\
               &=  \dfrac{6 \times 3}{3} \\\\
               &=  \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
               &=  6 \text{ miles/hour} \\\\
\text{Distance}&=  6 \times 2 \\\\
               &=  12 \text{ miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12 \text{ miles}$

</div>
</div>

               &=  \dfrac{6 \times 3}{3} \\\\
               &=  \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\

               &=  12 \text{ miles}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jason takes $4 \ \text{hours}$ to cycle $24 \ \text{kilometres}$. If Jason always cycles at the same speed, after how many hours has Jason cycled $15 \ \text{kilometres?}$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jason takes $4 \ \text{hours}$ to cycle $24 \ \text{kilometres}$. If Jason always cycles at the same speed, after how many hours has Jason cycled $15 \ \text{kilometres?}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}     &=  \dfrac{24}{4} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{6 \times 4}{4} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
                 &=  6 \text{ kilometres/hour} \\\\
\text{Time taken}&=  \dfrac{15}{6} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{3 \times 5}{3 \times 2} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 5}{\cancel{3} \times 2} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{5}{2} \\\\
                 &=  2.5 \text{ hours or } 2 \text{ hours and } 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2.5 \text{ hours or } 2 \text{ hours and } 30 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_speed
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}     &=  \dfrac{24}{4} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{6 \times 4}{4} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
                 &=  6 \text{ kilometres/hour} \\\\
\text{Time taken}&=  \dfrac{15}{6} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{3 \times 5}{3 \times 2} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 5}{\cancel{3} \times 2} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{5}{2} \\\\
                 &=  2.5 \text{ hours or } 2 \text{ hours and } 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jacqueline and Sophia stand facing one another. At exactly the same moment both girls start to turn steadily on the spot.

It takes Jaqueline $3 \ \text{seconds}$ to make one full turn, whilst Sophia takes $4 \ \text{seconds}$ to complete one full turn.

How many times will Jaqueline have turned when the girls are next facing each other? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jacqueline and Sophia stand facing one another. At exactly the same moment both girls start to turn steadily on the spot.
<emptyline>
It takes Jaqueline $3 \ \text{seconds}$ to make one full turn, whilst Sophia takes $4 \ \text{seconds}$ to complete one full turn.
<emptyline>
How many times will Jaqueline have turned when the girls are next facing each other? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

LCM of $3,4 = 12$

Time taken $= 12 \text{ seconds}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of turns}  &=  \dfrac{12}{3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac{3 \times 4}{3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 4}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
                        &=  4
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4$

</div>
</div>

                        &=  \dfrac{3 \times 4}{3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 4}{\cancel{3}} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A  space ship  travels  at  $193 \ \text{metres   per   second}$   for  $5  \ \text{minutes} \ 7 \ \text{seconds}$. Use   approximations to decide   whether  the   distance travelled   will  be nearer to $1 \ \text{km}, 10 \ \text{km}, 60 \ \text{km}$ or $600 \ \text{km}$. (You  must  show  your  working.) 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A  space ship  travels  at  $193 \ \text{metres   per   second}$   for  $5  \ \text{minutes} \ 7 \ \text{seconds}$. Use   approximations to decide   whether  the   distance travelled   will  be nearer to $1 \ \text{km}, 10 \ \text{km}, 60 \ \text{km}$ or $600 \ \text{km}$. (You  must  show  your  working.) 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance}  &=  193 \times (5 \text{ minutes } 7 \text{ seconds}) \\\\ 
                 &=  193 \times (5 \times 60 + 7) \\\\ 
                 &=  193 \times (300 + 7) \\\\ 
                 &=  193 \times 307 \\\\ 
                 &=  59251 \text{ metres} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  60000 \text{ metres} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  \dfrac{60000}{1000} \text{ km} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  \dfrac{60 \times 1000}{1000} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  \dfrac{60 \times \cancel{1000}}{\cancel{1000}} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  60 \text{ km}
\end{aligned}
$

The distance travelled will be nearer to $60$ km.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60$ km

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="workings">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If he takes $15 \ \text{minutes}$ to run $4 \ \text{km}$, what is his average speed in km 
per hour?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If he takes $15 \ \text{minutes}$ to run $4 \ \text{km}$, what is his average speed in km 
per hour?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}    &= \dfrac {4 \ \text{km}} {15  \ \text{minutes}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 60 \ \text{km}} {15  \ \text{hours}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 60} {15} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 15 \times 4} {15} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{15} \times 4} {\cancel{15}} \\\\
                &= 4 \times 4 \\\\
                &= 16 \ \text{km per hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16 \ \text{km per hour}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_speed
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$

\text{Speed}    &= \dfrac {4 \ \text{km}} {15  \ \text{minutes}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 60 \ \text{km}} {15  \ \text{hours}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 60} {15} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 15 \times 4} {15} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{15} \times 4} {\cancel{15}} \\\\
                &= 4 \times 4 \\\\
                &= 16 \ \text{km per hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Isobel cycles at a steady speed of $15 \ \text{miles per hour}$. Find the shortest time to complete the journey. 
Give your answer in minutes.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Isobel cycles at a steady speed of $15 \ \text{miles per hour}$. Find the shortest time to complete the journey. 
Give your answer in minutes.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time Isobel takes}     &= \dfrac {10} {15 \ \text {miles per hour}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {15 \ \text {miles per min}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {15} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 3 \times 20} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 2 \times \cancel 3 \times 20} {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 3} \\\\
                             &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                             &= 40 \ \text {min}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40 \ \text {min}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_speed
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}

                             &= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {15 \ \text {miles per min}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {15} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 3 \times 20} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 2 \times \cancel 3 \times 20} {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 3} \\\\
                             &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                             &= 40 \ \text {min}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Isobel listens to the local radio before setting off on her journey one morning. 
She discovers that the road between $E$ and $F$ is completely blocked due to an earlier accident 
and she is forced to choose an alternative route to work. 
What is the least number of **additional** minutes that she will take to cycle to work today?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Isobel listens to the local radio before setting off on her journey one morning. 
She discovers that the road between $E$ and $F$ is completely blocked due to an earlier accident 
and she is forced to choose an alternative route to work. 
What is the least number of **additional** minutes that she will take to cycle to work today?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If $E$ and $F$ are blocked the shortest new route would be:

From $S \ \text {to} \ B \ \text {to} \ C \ \text{to} \ T$ .

Let's find the new distance.

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Distance}  &= S \ \text{to} \ B \ \text{to} \ C \ \text{to} \ T \\\\
                  &= 4 + 2 + 6 \\\\
                  &= 12 \ \text {miles} \\\\
\text {Speed}     &= 15 \ \text {miles per hour \ \ } \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {15} {60} \ \text {miles per min}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time} & = \dfrac {12} { \dfrac {15}{60} } \\\\
            & = 12  \times \dfrac {60} {15} \\\\
            & = \dfrac {12 \times 60} {15} \\\\
            & = \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 5 \times 12} {5 \times 3} \\\\
            & = \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 4 \times \cancel 5 \times 12} {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 3} \\\\
            & = 4 \times 12 \\\\
            & = 48 \ \text {min}                                                                   
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the additional minutes by finding the difference in time taken by previous route and the new route.

$48 \ \text {min}  - 40 \ \text {min} = 8 \ \text {min} $                                                                  

The additional minutes Isobel will take to sycel to work today $= 8 \ \text {min}$ 
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8 \ \text {min}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

                  &= 4 + 2 + 6 \\\\
                  &= 12 \ \text {miles} \\\\
\text {Speed}     &= 15 \ \text {miles per hour \ \ } \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {15} {60} \ \text {miles per min}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time} & = \dfrac {12} { \dfrac {15}{60} } \\\\
            & = 12  \times \dfrac {60} {15} \\\\
            & = \dfrac {12 \times 60} {15} \\\\
            & = \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 5 \times 12} {5 \times 3} \\\\
            & = \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 4 \times \cancel 5 \times 12} {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 3} \\\\
            & = 4 \times 12 \\\\
            & = 48 \ \text {min}                                                                   
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Let's find the additional minutes by finding the difference in time taken by previous route and the new route.
<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="uuid">
<p>1D053FF5-F5C3-4A33-9F15-EF02705C8BD0</p>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Train $A$ leaves Birmingham New Street station at $1747$ travelling due North on the slow line at $90 \ \text{km/hour}$.

How many kilometres has this train travelled when the time is $1817?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Train $A$ leaves Birmingham New Street station at $1747$ travelling due North on the slow line at $90 \ \text{km/hour}$.
<emptyline>
How many kilometres has this train travelled when the time is $1817?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time taken}  &= 18{:}17 - 17{:}47 \\\\
                    &= 30 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Distance}     &= 30 \times 90 \text{ km/hour} \\\\
                    &= 30 \times \dfrac{90}{60} \text{ km/minutes} \\\\
                    &= 30 \times \dfrac{90}{60} \\\\
                    &= 30 \times \dfrac{3 \times 3 \times 10}{3 \times 2 \times 10} \\\\
                    &= 30 \times \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{10}}{\cancel{3} \times 2 \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{3 \times 30}{2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{3 \times 15 \times 2}{2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{3 \times 15 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                    &= 3 \times 15 \\\\
                    &= 45 \text{ km}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$45\text{ km}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}

                    &= 30 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Distance}     &= 30 \times 90 \text{ km/hour} \\\\
                    &= 30 \times \dfrac{90}{60} \text{ km/minutes} \\\\
                    &= 30 \times \dfrac{90}{60} \\\\
                    &= 30 \times \dfrac{3 \times 3 \times 10}{3 \times 2 \times 10} \\\\
                    &= 30 \times \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{10}}{\cancel{3} \times 2 \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{3 \times 30}{2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{3 \times 15 \times 2}{2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{3 \times 15 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                    &= 3 \times 15 \\\\
                    &= 45 \text{ km}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>

</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$45\text{ km}$
</code></pre>
</div>
