<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Train $B$ leaves Birmingham New Street station at $1817$ also travelling due North but on the fast line. 
The speed of train $B$ is $135 \ \text{km/hour}$.

At what time does train $B$ overtake train $A$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Train $B$ leaves Birmingham New Street station at $1817$ also travelling due North but on the fast line. 
The speed of train $B$ is $135 \ \text{km/hour}$.
<emptyline>
At what time does train $B$ overtake train $A$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the time taken (in minutes) by trian A to get overtaken by trian B $= t$

$
\begin{aligned}
135 \text{ km/hour} \times (t - 30)                                       &=  90 \text{ km/hour} \times t \\\\
\dfrac{135}{60} \text{ km/minute} \times (t - 30)                         &=  \dfrac{90}{60} \text{ km/minute} \times t \\\\
\dfrac{135}{60} \times (t - 30)                                           &=  \dfrac{90}{60} \times t \\\\
\dfrac{135 \times (t - 30)}{60}                                           &=  \dfrac{90 \times t}{60} \\\\
\dfrac{135 \times (t - 30)}{60} \times 60                                 &=  90t \\\\
\dfrac{135 \times (t - 30)}{\cancel{60}} \times \cancel{60}               &=  90t \\\\
135 \times (t - 30)                                                       &=  90t \\\\
135t - 4050                                                               &=  90t \\\\
135t - 90t                                                                &=  4050 \\\\
45t                                                                       &=  4050 \\\\
t                                                                         &=  \dfrac{4050}{45} \\\\
t                                                                         &=  \dfrac{90 \times 9 \times 5}{9 \times 5} \\\\
t                                                                         &=  \dfrac{90 \times \cancel{9} \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{9} \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
t                                                                         &=  90 \text { minutes} \\\\
t                                                                         &=  1 \text { hour and } 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}   &=   17{:}47 + 1 \text { hours and } 30 \text{ minutes} \\\\
              &=   19{:}17
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$19{:}17$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the time taken (in minutes) by trian A to get overtaken by trian B $= t$

$

\dfrac{135}{60} \text{ km/minute} \times (t - 30)                         &=  \dfrac{90}{60} \text{ km/minute} \times t \\\\
\dfrac{135}{60} \times (t - 30)                                           &=  \dfrac{90}{60} \times t \\\\
\dfrac{135 \times (t - 30)}{60}                                           &=  \dfrac{90 \times t}{60} \\\\
\dfrac{135 \times (t - 30)}{60} \times 60                                 &=  90t \\\\

135t - 4050                                                               &=  90t \\\\

t                                                                         &=  90 \text { minutes} \\\\
t                                                                         &=  1 \text { hour and } 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}

              &=   19{:}17

<div class="question_envelope rag_pr rag_prns question">
<div class="uuid">
<p>10B39F70-A4DD-4349-8C17-8BB01B8438F1</p>
</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school--11-plus--maths--2014--sample-paper-5:1:20</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_pr</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_prns</p>

<div class="topics">
<ul>
<li>
tspeed
</li>
<li>
ttime
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A tortoise and a hare take part in a race which has a staggered start in order to make the race fair.

The hare needs to run a distance of $400 \ \text{metres}$ to cross the finishing line whereas the tortoise 
only needs to travel $1.5 \ \text{metres}$. The hare runs at a speed of $800 \ \text{metres}$ per minute. 
At what speed (in metres per minute) does the tortoise need to run to cross the finishing line at the same time as the hare?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A tortoise and a hare take part in a race which has a staggered start in order to make the race fair.
<emptyline>
The hare needs to run a distance of $400 \ \text{metres}$ to cross the finishing line whereas the tortoise 
only needs to travel $1.5 \ \text{metres}$. The hare runs at a speed of $800 \ \text{metres}$ per minute. 
At what speed (in metres per minute) does the tortoise need to run to cross the finishing line at the same time as the hare?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by hare}  &=  \dfrac{400}{800} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{4 \times 100}{2 \times 4 \times 100} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{\cancel{4} \times \cancel{100}}{2 \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{100}} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{1}{2} \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Tortoise speed}      &=  \dfrac{1.5}{\dfrac{1}{2}} \\\\
                           &=  1.5 \times 2 \\\\
                           &=  3 \text { metres per minute}
\end{aligned}
$

At $3 \text { metres per minute}$, the tortoise need to run to cross the finishing line at the same time as the hare.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3 \text { metres per minute}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance_time
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by hare}  &=  \dfrac{400}{800} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{4 \times 100}{2 \times 4 \times 100} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{\cancel{4} \times \cancel{100}}{2 \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{100}} \\\\
                           &=  \dfrac{1}{2} \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Tortoise speed}      &=  \dfrac{1.5}{\dfrac{1}{2}} \\\\
                           &=  1.5 \times 2 \\\\
                           &=  3 \text { metres per minute}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
At $3 \text { metres per minute}$, the tortoise need to run to cross the finishing line at the same time as the hare.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Andy walks $2.4 \ \text{kilometres}$ in $30 \ \text{minutes}$. 
Work out his speed in both kilometres per hour and metres per minute.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Andy walks $2.4 \ \text{kilometres}$ in $30 \ \text{minutes}$. 
Work out his speed in both kilometres per hour and metres per minute.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Speed}       &= \dfrac {2.4} {30 \text{ minutes}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {\dfrac{30}{60} \text{ hour}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {\dfrac{30}{60}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {\dfrac{30}{30 \times 2}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {\dfrac{\cancel{30}}{\cancel{30} \times 2}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {\dfrac{1}{2}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {0.5} \\\\
                    &= 4.8 \text{ kilometres/hour} \\\\
                    &= 4.8 \times \dfrac{1000}{60} \text{ metres/minute} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{4.8 \times 1000}{60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{4800}{60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{480\cancel{0}}{6\cancel{0}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{480}{6} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{80 \times 6}{6} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{80 \times \cancel{6}}{\cancel{6}} \\\\
                    &= 80 \text{ metres/minute} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4.8 \text{ kilometres/hour}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$80 \text{ metres/minute}$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {\dfrac{30}{60}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {\dfrac{30}{30 \times 2}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {\dfrac{\cancel{30}}{\cancel{30} \times 2}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {\dfrac{1}{2}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2.4} {0.5} \\\\
                    &= 4.8 \text{ kilometres/hour} \\\\
                    &= 4.8 \times \dfrac{1000}{60} \text{ metres/minute} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{4.8 \times 1000}{60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{4800}{60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{480\cancel{0}}{6\cancel{0}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{480}{6} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{80 \times 6}{6} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{80 \times \cancel{6}}{\cancel{6}} \\\\
                    &= 80 \text{ metres/minute} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A sunflower seed takes a week to germinate after it has been sown in the ground. After germination it grows the same number centimetres each day.

Maya sows a sunflower seed. She measures the height of the plant at midday on the $16 ^\text{th}$ 
August and again at midday on the $21 ^\text{st}$ August. These
two measurements are recorded as $48 \ \text{cm}$ and $68 \ \text{cm}$ respectively.

<div class="question_envelope rag_notstarted rag_prns question">
<div class="uuid">
<p>77F62AEF-602C-4E6C-8EBE-202B89E04308</p>
</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2016--group-1:1:44</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_notstarted</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_prns</p>
</div>
<div class="topics">
<ul>
<li>
tspeed
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the same time that Tom can run $100 \ \text{metres}$, Jerry can run only $60 \ \text{metres}$.

Tom and Jerry have a competition.

When Jerry has run $100 \ \text{metres}$, Tom sets off in pursuit.

How many metres will Tom run before he catches Jerry?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the same time that Tom can run $100 \ \text{metres}$, Jerry can run only $60 \ \text{metres}$.
<emptyline>
Tom and Jerry have a competition.
<emptyline>
When Jerry has run $100 \ \text{metres}$, Tom sets off in pursuit.
<emptyline>
How many metres will Tom run before he catches Jerry?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the distance covered by Tom $= a$

Distance covered by Jerry $= a - 100$

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
100 \ \text{m} : 60 \ \text{m}                  &= a \ \text{m} : (a - 100) \ \text{m} \\\\
\dfrac{100} {60}                                &= \dfrac {a} {a - 100} \\\\
100 \times (a - 100)                            &= a \times 60 \\\\
100a - 10000                                    &= 60a \\\\
100a - 60a                                      &= 10000 \\\\
40a                                             &= 10000 \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {10000} {40} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {40 \times 250} {40} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {\cancel {40} \times 250} {\cancel {40}} \\\\
a                                               &= 250 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$250 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the distance covered by Tom $= a$
<emptyline>
Distance covered by Jerry $= a - 100$
<emptyline>
Using equivalent ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
100 \ \text{m} : 60 \ \text{m}                  &= a \ \text{m} : (a - 100) \ \text{m} \\\\
\dfrac{100} {60}                                &= \dfrac {a} {a - 100} \\\\
100 \times (a - 100)                            &= a \times 60 \\\\
100a - 10000                                    &= 60a \\\\
100a - 60a                                      &= 10000 \\\\
40a                                             &= 10000 \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {10000} {40} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {40 \times 250} {40} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {\cancel {40} \times 250} {\cancel {40}} \\\\
a                                               &= 250 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$250 \ \text{m}$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two icebergs, $A$ and $B$, are floating in the ocean.

On $1$ January, iceberg $A$ weighs $4 \ \text{tonnes}$, but loses $25 \ \text{kg}$ every day.

$1000 \ \text{kg} = 1 \ \text{tonne}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

After how many days will iceberg $A$ weigh $3850 \ \text{kg}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">After how many days will iceberg $A$ weigh $3850 \ \text{kg}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>

**Formulas**

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Lost weight}  &= 4000 - 3850 \\\\
                    &= 150 \text{ kg}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of days}  &= \dfrac{150}{25} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{25 \times 6}{25} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{\cancel{25} \times 6}{\cancel{25}} \\\\
                       &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On $1$ January, iceberg $B$ weighs $4500 \ \text{kg}$ and loses $50 \ \text{kg}$ every day.

After how many days will the two icebergs have the same mass?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On $1$ January, iceberg $B$ weighs $4500 \ \text{kg}$ and loses $50 \ \text{kg}$ every day.
<emptyline>
After how many days will the two icebergs have the same mass?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of days $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4000 - 25 \times a  &=  4500 - 50 \times a  \\\\
4000 - 25a          &=  4500 - 50a  \\\\
50a - 25a           &=  4500 - 4000  \\\\
25a                 &=  500  \\\\
a                   &=  \dfrac{500}{25}  \\\\
a                   &=  \dfrac{20 \times \cancel{25}}{\cancel{25}}  \\\\
a                   &=  20
\end{aligned}
$

After $20$ days, the two icebergs will have the same mass.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_g2 rag_prns question">
<div class="uuid">
<p>1726FC9C-67EA-46CA-9AE2-A3CB7D96F149</p>
</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>the-manchester-grammar-school--11-plus--maths--2010--arithmetic-1:1:9</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_g2</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_prns</p>

<div class="topics">

tspeed
</li>

tunitprompt
</li>

</div>
<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Adam ran $3 \ \text{kilometres}$ in $13 \ \text{minutes}$. Jackie started $30 \ \text{seconds}$ after him and finished $30 \  \text{seconds}$ before him. What was Jackie's speed in kilometres per hour?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Adam ran $3 \ \text{kilometres}$ in $13 \ \text{minutes}$. Jackie started $30 \ \text{seconds}$ after him and finished $30 \  \text{seconds}$ before him. What was Jackie's speed in kilometres per hour?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Jackie}   &= 13 \ \text{minutes} - 30 \ \text{seconds} - 30 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                              &= 13 \ \text{minutes} - 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                              &= 13 \ \text{minutes} - 1 \ \text{minute} \\\\
                              &= 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Speed}                  &= \dfrac {3 \text{ kilometres}} {12 \ \text{minutes}} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {3 \times 60 \text{ kilometres}} {12 \ \text{hours}} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {3 \times 60} {12} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {3 \times 12 \times 5} {12} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{12} \times 5} {\cancel{12}} \\\\
                              &= 3 \times 5 \\\\
                              &= 15 \text{ kilometres per hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$15 \text{ kilometres per hour}$

</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Jackie}   &= 13 \ \text{minutes} - 30 \ \text{seconds} - 30 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                              &= 13 \ \text{minutes} - 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                              &= 13 \ \text{minutes} - 1 \ \text{minute} \\\\
                              &= 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Speed}                  &= \dfrac {3 \text{ kilometres}} {12 \ \text{minutes}} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {3 \times 60 \text{ kilometres}} {12 \ \text{hours}} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {3 \times 60} {12} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {3 \times 12 \times 5} {12} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{12} \times 5} {\cancel{12}} \\\\
                              &= 3 \times 5 \\\\
                              &= 15 \text{ kilometres per hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A distance of $8 \ \text{furlongs}$ is equal to $1 \ \text{mile}$. 

A horse runs a $5  \ \text{furlong}$ race in a time of $62.5  \ \text{seconds}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

<emptyline>
A horse runs a $5  \ \text{furlong}$ race in a time of $62.5  \ \text{seconds}$.
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

At what speed is the horse running, in miles per hour?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">At what speed is the horse running, in miles per hour?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed} &= \dfrac{1}{100} \text{ miles per second} \\\\
             &= \dfrac{1}{100} \times 60 \times 60 \text{ miles per hour} \\\\
             &= \dfrac{60 \times 60}{100} \\\\
             &= \dfrac{6 \times 10 \times 6 \times 10}{10 \times 10} \\\\
             &= \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{10} \times 6 \times \cancel{10}}{\cancel{10} \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
             &= 6 \times 6 \\\\
             &= 36 \text { miles/hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$36 \text { miles/hour}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Neil drives his car for $20 \ \text{minutes}$ at $45 \ \text{kilometres per hour}$.

Stephen does the same journey at a speed of $30 \ \text{kilometres per hour}$. How long did it take him?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Neil drives his car for $20 \ \text{minutes}$ at $45 \ \text{kilometres per hour}$.
<emptyline>
Stephen does the same journey at a speed of $30 \ \text{kilometres per hour}$. How long did it take him?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance}                 &= 45 \text{ kilometres} \times 20 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 45 \text{ kilometres} \times \dfrac{20}{60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {45 \times 20} {60}  \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {15 \times 3 \times 20} {20 \times 3}  \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {15 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{20}} {\cancel{20} \times \cancel{3}}  \\\\
                                &= 15 \text{ kilometres}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Stephen}    &= \dfrac {15}{30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {15}{15 \times 2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{15}}{\cancel{15} \times 2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1}{2} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {60}{2} \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$30 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance}                 &= 45 \text{ kilometres} \times 20 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 45 \text{ kilometres} \times \dfrac{20}{60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {45 \times 20} {60}  \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {15 \times 3 \times 20} {20 \times 3}  \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {15 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{20}} {\cancel{20} \times \cancel{3}}  \\\\
                                &= 15 \text{ kilometres}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Stephen}    &= \dfrac {15}{30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {15}{15 \times 2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{15}}{\cancel{15} \times 2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1}{2} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {60}{2} \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$30 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

When a ball is dropped, the relationship between the speed ($S$) of the ball in $\text{m/s}$ and the distance ($d$) in $\text{m}$ it has fallen is

$S^2 = 20 \times d$ where $S^2 = S \times S$

So after dropping $5 \ \text{m}$, $S^2 = 20 \times 5 = 100$ and therefore the speed would be $10 \ \text{m/s}$ as $10 \times 10 = 100$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">When a ball is dropped, the relationship between the speed ($S$) of the ball in $\text{m/s}$ and the distance ($d$) in $\text{m}$ it has fallen is
<emptyline>
$S^2 = 20 \times d$ where $S^2 = S \times S$
<emptyline>
So after dropping $5 \ \text{m}$, $S^2 = 20 \times 5 = 100$ and therefore the speed would be $10 \ \text{m/s}$ as $10 \times 10 = 100$.
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the speed when the ball has dropped $45 \ \text{m}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the speed when the ball has dropped $45 \ \text{m}$.
</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

When the speed is $40 \ \text{m/s}$, how far has the ball fallen?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">When the speed is $40 \ \text{m/s}$, how far has the ball fallen?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
S^2          &= 20 \times d \\\\
40^2         &= 20 \times d \\\\
20 \times d  &= 40 \times 40 \\\\
d            &= \dfrac {40 \times 40}{20} \\\\
d            &= \dfrac {40 \times 20 \times 2}{20} \\\\
d            &= \dfrac {40 \times \cancel {20} \times 2}{\cancel {20}} \\\\
d            &= 40 \times 2 \\\\
d            &= 80 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$80 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

How much further would the ball have to fall to be travelling at twice the speed that the ball had in part (b)?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much further would the ball have to fall to be travelling at twice the speed that the ball had in part (b)?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}  &= 40 \times 2 \\\\
              &= 80  \ \text{m/s}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
S^2         &= 20 \times d \\\\
80^2        &= 20 \times d \\\\
20 \times d &= 80 \times 80 \\\\
d           &= \dfrac {80 \times 80}{20} \\\\
d           &= \dfrac {80 \times 20 \times 4}{20} \\\\
d           &= \dfrac {80 \times \cancel{20} \times 4}{\cancel {20}} \\\\    
d           &= 4 \times 80 \\\\
d           &= 320 \ \text{m} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$240 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>

tspeed
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The stopping distance for a car is made up of two parts. 

The first is the distance travelled by the car while the driver is reacting to something that makes them want to brake. 
This is called the Thinking Distance. 

The second is the distance travelled by the car while the brakes are applied. This is called the Braking Distance. 

The Stopping Distance is given by adding these two distances together.

So Stopping Distance=Thinking Distance + Braking Distance

The table below shows the Thinking Distance, in metres, for various speeds in km per hour.

{/*
$\text{Speed (kmph)}$,$\text{Thinking Distance (m)}$
$40$, $12$
$50$, $15$
$60$, $18$
$70$, $21$
$80$, $24$
$90$, $27$
*/}

| Speed ($\text{kmph}$) | Thinking Distance ($\text{m}$) |
|:---------------------:|:------------------------------:|
|          $40$         |              $12$              |
|          $50$         |              $15$              |
|          $60$         |              $18$              |
|          $70$         |              $21$              |
|          $80$         |              $24$              |
|          $90$         |              $27$              |

The Braking Distance in metres is given by the formula:

$\text{Braking Distance} = \dfrac{1}{60} \times \text{speed} \times \text{speed}$ or $= \dfrac{1}{60} \times (\text{speed})^2$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>

**Question**

What is the Braking Distance of a car travelling at $90 \ \text{kmph}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the Braking Distance of a car travelling at $90 \ \text{kmph}?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Braking distance}    &= \dfrac{1}{60} \times 90 \times 90 \\\\
                           &= \dfrac{90 \times 90}{60} \\\\
                           &= \dfrac{\cancel{30} \times 3 \times 45 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{30} \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
                           &= 3 \times 45 \\\\
                           &= 135 \text{ m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$135 \text{ m}$

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A driver sees a child start to cross the road $80 \ \text{m}$ in front of his car. 
What distance would the car be from the child when the driver stopped if he was initially travelling at $60 \ \text{kmph}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A driver sees a child start to cross the road $80 \ \text{m}$ in front of his car. 
What distance would the car be from the child when the driver stopped if he was initially travelling at $60 \ \text{kmph}?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the table,

Thinking distance  $= 18 \text{ m}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Braking distance}                     &= \dfrac{1}{60} \times 60 \times 60 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac{60 \times 60}{60} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac{\cancel{60} \times 60}{\cancel{60}} \\\\
                                            &= 60 \text{ m} \\\\
\text{Stopping distance}                    &= 60 + 18 \\\\
                                            &= 78 \text{ m} \\\\    
\text{distance between car and child}       &=  80 -  78 \\\\
                                            &=  2 \text{ m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \text{ m}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The distance, $d$, in metres, travelled by any vehicle accelerating at a rate $A$, in $t$ seconds is given by the formula

$d = A \times t^2 \div 2$ or $d = A \times t \times t \div 2$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The distance, $d$, in metres, travelled by any vehicle accelerating at a rate $A$, in $t$ seconds is given by the formula
<emptyline>
$d = A \times t^2 \div 2$ or $d = A \times t \times t \div 2$
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the distance travelled by a car accelerating at a rate of $8$ for $3 \ \text{seconds}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the distance travelled by a car accelerating at a rate of $8$ for $3 \ \text{seconds}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance}   &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 3}{2} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {4 \times 2 \times 9}{2} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{2} \times 9}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                  &= 4 \times 9 \\\\
                  &= 36 \text { metres}
\end{aligned}
$

Distance is $36 \text { metres}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$36 \text { metres}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

**Question**

Find the distance travelled by a space rocket accelerating at a rate of $20$ for $2 \ \text{minutes}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the distance travelled by a space rocket accelerating at a rate of $20$ for $2 \ \text{minutes}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}       &= 2 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                  &= 2 \times 60 \text{ seconds} \\\\
                  &= 2 \times 60 \\\\
                  &= 120 \text{ seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance}   &= \dfrac {20 \times 120 \times 120}{2} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {10 \times 2 \times 120 \times 120}{2} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {10 \times \cancel{2} \times 120 \times 120}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                  &= 10 \times 120 \times 120 \\\\
                  &= 144000 \text { metres}
\end{aligned}
$

Distance is $144000 \ \text {metres}$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A motorbike travels $270 \ \text{metres}$ in $6 \ \text{seconds}$.  What is its rate of acceleration?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A motorbike travels $270 \ \text{metres}$ in $6 \ \text{seconds}$.  What is its rate of acceleration?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the acceleration $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a \times 6 \times 6}{2}                                     &= 270 \\\\
\dfrac {a \times 6 \times 3 \times 2}{2}                            &= 270 \\\\
\dfrac {a \times 6 \times 3 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}}          &= 270 \\\\
a \times 6 \times 3                                                 &= 270 \\\\
18a                                                                 &= 270 \\\\
a                                                                   &= \dfrac{270}{18} \\\\
a                                                                   &= \dfrac{15 \times 9 \times 2}{9 \times 2} \\\\
a                                                                   &= \dfrac{15 \times \cancel{9} \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{9} \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
a                                                                   &= 15
\end{aligned}
$

Rate of acceleration $= 15$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$15$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A truck travels $50 \ \text{metres}$ while accelerating at a rate of $4$. How long does this take?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A truck travels $50 \ \text{metres}$ while accelerating at a rate of $4$. How long does this take?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the time (in seconds) $= t$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {4 \times t^2}{2}                                     &= 50 \\\\
\dfrac {2 \times 2 \times t^2}{2}                            &= 50 \\\\
\dfrac {2 \times \cancel{2} \times t^2}{\cancel{2}}          &= 50 \\\\
2t^2                                                         &= 50 \\\\
t^2                                                          &= \dfrac{50}{2} \\\\
t^2                                                          &= \dfrac{25 \times 2}{2} \\\\
t^2                                                          &= \dfrac{25 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
t^2                                                          &= 25 \\\\
t                                                            &= \sqrt{25} \\\\
t                                                            &= 5 \text{ seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

Time taken $ = 5$ seconds.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a school athletics triathlon, children score points for their performances in three events, running $100 \ \text{m}$, Long Jump and Shot Putt (throwing) according to the following table:

|$100$ $\text{m}$ ($\text{seconds}$) |Long Jump ($\text{metres}$) |Shot Putt ($\text{metres}$)| Points|
|:--:|:--:|:--:|:--:|
|$16.2$|$2.2$ |$7.2$ |$50$ |
|$14.9$|$2.35$|$7.5$ |$60$ |  
|$14.1$|$2.7$ |$8.0$ |$70$ |
|$13.5$|$3.1$ |$8.6$ |$80$ |
|$13.2$|$3.55$|$9.5$ |$90$ |
|$13.0$|$4.1$ |$11.8$|$100$|

So if Richard ran the $100 \ \text{m}$ in $13.0 \ \text{secs}$ he would earn $100$ points, he would earn a further 
$80$ points for putting the shot $8.6 \ \text{m}$ and $70$ points for a long jump of $2.7 \ \text{m}$ making a total of 
$250$ points scored for his performance in the three events.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a school athletics triathlon, children score points for their performances in three events, running $100 \ \text{m}$, Long Jump and Shot Putt (throwing) according to the following table:

|$14.9$|$2.35$|$7.5$ |$60$ |  
|$14.1$|$2.7$ |$8.0$ |$70$ |
|$13.5$|$3.1$ |$8.6$ |$80$ |
|$13.2$|$3.55$|$9.5$ |$90$ |
|$13.0$|$4.1$ |$11.8$|$100$|
<emptyline>
So if Richard ran the $100 \ \text{m}$ in $13.0 \ \text{secs}$ he would earn $100$ points, he would earn a further 
$80$ points for putting the shot $8.6 \ \text{m}$ and $70$ points for a long jump of $2.7 \ \text{m}$ making a total of 
$250$ points scored for his performance in the three events.
</code></pre>

**Question**

Deepak averages $60$ points over the three events having run the $100 \ \text{m}$ in $16.2 \ \text{secs}$ and 
long jumped $2.2 \ \text{metres}$. How far does he putt the shot?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Deepak averages $60$ points over the three events having run the $100 \ \text{m}$ in $16.2 \ \text{secs}$ and 
long jumped $2.2 \ \text{metres}$. How far does he putt the shot?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the points scored in shot putt $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{50 + 50 + a}{3}   &=  60 \\\\
\dfrac{100 + a}{3}       &=  60 \\\\
100 + a                  &=  60 \times 3 \\\\
100 + a                  &=  180 \\\\
a                        &=  180 - 100 \\\\
a                        &=  80
\end{aligned}
$

Deepak putts the shot $8.6$ m
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8.6$ m

</div>
</div>

**Question**

A Gold award is achieved by scoring a total of $270$ points.  What is the **slowest** run that could win a Gold award?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A Gold award is achieved by scoring a total of $270$ points.  What is the **slowest** run that could win a Gold award?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Points scored in } 100 \text{ m}    &= 270 - 100 - 100 \\\\
                                     &= 270 - 200 \\\\
                                     &= 70
\end{aligned}
$

slowest run $= 14.1$ secs
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$14.1$ secs

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A top international bowler playing in a cricket match bowls a cricket ball at $90  \ \text{miles per hour}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A top international bowler playing in a cricket match bowls a cricket ball at $90  \ \text{miles per hour}$.
</code></pre>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many miles would the ball travel in one minute?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many miles would the ball travel in one minute?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Distance} &= 90{ miles/hour} \times 1 \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{90}{60} \text{ miles/minute} \times 1 \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{90}{60} \times 1 \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{90}{60} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{10}}{\cancel{3} \times 2 \times \cancel{10}} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac {3}{2} \\\\
                 &= 1.5 \text { miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1.5 \text { miles}$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If the distance the ball travels through the air from the point where the bowler bowls the ball to the point where it is hit by the batsman is $22  \ \text{yards}$, how long is the ball travelling for?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the distance the ball travels through the air from the point where the bowler bowls the ball to the point where it is hit by the batsman is $22  \ \text{yards}$, how long is the ball travelling for?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time}     &= \dfrac{22}{44} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{22}{22 \times 2} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{\cancel{22}}{\cancel{22} \times 2} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{1}{2} \\\\
                 &= 0.5 \text { seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$0.5$ seconds

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

One length of my local swimming pool is $25\, \text{m}$.

How many lengths must I swim to cover a distance of $1.5\, \text{km}$? 

![papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q07-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q07-i00.png "papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q07-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">One length of my local swimming pool is $25\, \text{m}$.
<emptyline>
How many lengths must I swim to cover a distance of $1.5\, \text{km}$? 
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of lengths}  &= \dfrac{1.5 \text{ km}}{25} \\\\ 
                          &= \dfrac{1.5 \times 1000 \text{ m}}{25} \\\\ 
                          &= \dfrac{1500}{25} \\\\ 
                          &= \dfrac{5 \times 5 \times 60}{5 \times 5} \\\\ 
                          &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{5} \times 60}{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{5}} \\\\ 
                          &= 60
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If it takes me $45\, \text{minutes}$ to swim the $1.5\, \text{km}$ how long would it take me to swim $1 \ \text{km}$ if I swim at the same speed?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If it takes me $45\, \text{minutes}$ to swim the $1.5\, \text{km}$ how long would it take me to swim $1 \ \text{km}$ if I swim at the same speed?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Speed}    &= \dfrac{1.5}{45} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{1.5 \times 10}{45 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{15}{15 \times 3 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{\cancel{15}}{\cancel{15} \times 3 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{1}{3 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{1}{30} \text{ km/minute}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time taken to swim } 1 \text{ km}    &= \dfrac{1}{\dfrac{1}{30}} \\\\
                                            &= 30 \text { minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$30 \text { minutes}$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$

                 &= \dfrac{1.5 \times 10}{45 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{15}{15 \times 3 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{\cancel{15}}{\cancel{15} \times 3 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{1}{3 \times 10} \\\\
                 &= \dfrac{1}{30} \text{ km/minute}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time taken to swim } 1 \text{ km}    &= \dfrac{1}{\dfrac{1}{30}} \\\\
                                            &= 30 \text { minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

For a certain journey, I always start at the same time in the morning and arrive later in the day.

If I travel at $10\ \text{km per hour}$, I arrive at $1.00\ \text{pm}$.

If I travel at $15\ \text{km per hour}$, I arrive at $11\ \text{am}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">For a certain journey, I always start at the same time in the morning and arrive later in the day.
<emptyline>
If I travel at $10\ \text{km per hour}$, I arrive at $1.00\ \text{pm}$.
<emptyline>
If I travel at $15\ \text{km per hour}$, I arrive at $11\ \text{am}$.
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How far is the journey?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How far is the journey?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the time taken in first case (in hours)  $= t$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
15 \times (t - 2)   &=  10 \times t \\\\
15t - 30            &=  10t \\\\
15t - 10t           &=  30 \\\\
5t                  &=  30 \\\\
t                   &=  \dfrac{30}{5} \\\\
t                   &=  \dfrac{6 \times 5}{5} \\\\
t                   &=  \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
t                   &=  6 \text{ hours} \\\\
\text{Distance}     &= 10 \times t \\\\
                    &= 10 \times 6 \\\\
                    &= 60 \text{ km}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60 \text{ km}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

What should my speed be (with the same start time) if I want to arrive at $12.00$ noon?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What should my speed be (with the same start time) if I want to arrive at $12.00$ noon?
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time Taken} &=  7{:}00 - 12{:}00 \\\\
                  &=  5 \text{ hours} \\\\
\text{Speed}      &=  \dfrac{60}{5} \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{12 \times 5}{5} \\\\
                  &=  \dfrac{12 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
                  &=  12 \text{ km per hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alan and Brian are two runners. Alan runs at $6.6\, \text{m/s}$ and Brian runs at $8.4\, \text{m/s}$. They start from the same place on a circular $400\, \text{m}$ track and run in opposite directions round and round the track.
 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Alan and Brian are two runners. Alan runs at $6.6\, \text{m/s}$ and Brian runs at $8.4\, \text{m/s}$. They start from the same place on a circular $400\, \text{m}$ track and run in opposite directions round and round the track.
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>




<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How far has Brian run when they pass each other for the first time?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How far has Brian run when they pass each other for the first time?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Time} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Speed}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the time taken (in seconds) $= t$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
6.6 \times t + 8.4 \times t &= 400 \\\\
6.6t + 8.4t                 &= 400 \\\\
15t                         &= 400 \\\\
t                           &= \dfrac{400}{15} \text{ seconds}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance covered by brian} &= \dfrac{400}{15} \times 8.4 \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{40 \times 10 \times 8.4}{15} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{40 \times 84}{15} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{5 \times 8 \times 28 \times 3}{5 \times 3} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 8 \times 28 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
                                 &= 8 \times 28 \\\\
                                 &= 224 \text{ metres}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$224$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_distance_time
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the time taken (in seconds) $= t$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
6.6 \times t + 8.4 \times t &= 400 \\\\
6.6t + 8.4t                 &= 400 \\\\
15t                         &= 400 \\\\
t                           &= \dfrac{400}{15} \text{ seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance covered by brian} &= \dfrac{400}{15} \times 8.4 \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{40 \times 10 \times 8.4}{15} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{40 \times 84}{15} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{5 \times 8 \times 28 \times 3}{5 \times 3} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 8 \times 28 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
                                 &= 8 \times 28 \\\\
                                 &= 224 \text{ metres}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
